ParlaMint-PL_2015-11-18-sejm-01-4.u42	Mr. Marshal! High Izbo! Prime Minister! The Prime Minister has arrived. I'm very happy for you. The construction of the Polish defence power is a permanent imperative resulting from its geopolitical position. It's an inalienable condition for her survival on the map of the world. Power is a force that provides you with the opportunity to pursue your own interests. The Armed Forces play a key role in building power. At the beginning of your speech, Prime Minister, you pointed out that ensuring the security of Poland would be an absolute priority for the new government. Unfortunately, in the national defence section, the Prime Minister limited herself to the declaration that the Armed Forces should be strengthened, that your government will seek to expand them. You also mentioned the need to rebuild the Polish arms industry and to strengthen the Eastern NATO flank. As the Kukiz Posel Club15, we want to say clearly here: In the situation in which Poland is currently located, it is far too little. Today, when international order is destabilized, when there is a war beyond our eastern border, when Europe is flooded with foreign immigrants culturally and civilly, Poland's ability to deter, protect and defend itself is a fundamental issue. However, the diagnosis of the ability of the Armed Forces of the Republic of Poland does not allow us to be optimistic. Between 2007 and 2012, the political decision-makers responsible for determining directions for the development of the national security system departed from the priority of strengthening defence capabilities to increase the ability of the Polish army to participate in allied operations. Under the slogans of the professionalization of the army and the abolition of forced conscription, the destruction of the military organization of our country was carried out. And so operating troops were minimized to only three divisions. This is the amount that fits peacefully in one stadium. At the same time, Polish soldiers are forced to leave for a civilian after 12 years. Military training of young people and reservists has been discontinued. The foundations of territorial defence have also been destroyed, destroying the structure built since the 1990s. Also sold as much as 20% military infrastructure. Military experts have made it clear that our army is currently not capable of effectively defending its territory. We are counting on NATO to protect us, but NATO is us, NATO's troops are mostly Polish troops in our territory. Prime Minister, in order to strengthen Poland's real defence capacity, it is necessary to rely on two basic pillars: firstly, the reconstruction of operational troops, secondly, the reconstruction of territorial defence. Territorial defence is a kind of armed force designed to conduct on-site defence. It consists of soldiers of citizens who are trained and armed and always ready to defend their families, homes, cities and villages. Territorial defence is also necessary to ensure the adoption of NATO support forces in the event of a possible armed conflict in our territory. Territorial defence is also an essential element for maneuvering and impacting operational forces. Currently, Poland has no such armed forces and they are an integral part of democratic, well-organized countries such as the USA, Israel, Switzerland, Finland and Sweden. That is why I ask the Prime Minister: Will you restore territorial defence as a second component of the Armed Forces? Will the potential of pro-defence organisations to build territorial defence structures be exploited? Will the number of armed forces divisions be increased and will they be located on the eastern wall of Poland? Will a drain from the Armed Forces of experienced soldiers be stopped, who today, after 12 years of service, are forced to leave for a civilian? Will the uniform chain of command be restored by the Armed Forces, because the current situation in which several decision-making centers are present as generating chaos is simply unacceptable? Finally, I would like to ask you about a matter which does not concern the strictly armed forces, but which is closely related to national security. Will your government, Prime Minister Beata Szydło's government, withdraw from the commitments of the predecessors regarding admission to Poland of foreign immigrants from culturally and civilised circles? [[Applause]] We expect a clear declaration. Thank you.
